# Decisions

Newest first. Entries dated before the 2026-10-05 restart come from the v1 and v2 prototypes (consolidated from `handoff/`); they hold unless marked "Replaced by". Things not yet decided live in `ROADMAP.md` as "Decide:", never here. An "owner verdict" on a prototype is feedback, not an adopted design.

## 2026-10-30 · Selected-state styles: Pedro's pick from «Selectores sin celeste» (Pedro chose; to try, not built yet)
**Pedro, after reviewing https://claude.ai/artifact/X68vN9LQjDD6JpfbmcGMxN (v1, archived in `docs/research/proposals/selectors/`):** «por ahora vamos a probar implementar los siguientes estilos: Filas y círculos: Marca corta; Pestañas: Elevado; Segmentos: Tinta llena; Fichas: Elevado».
- **Why:** outside feedback called the current recipe («`accent-weak` fill + inset 2 px accent bar» on rows, underline on tabs, fill on segments) «AI-SLOP»; research and the six reference sidebars are in `docs/features/selectors-proposal.md`.
- **Rows and circles, «Marca corta»:** a 14 × 2 px ink dash next to the text instead of the fill and the full-height bar; on the chapter grid the same dash replaces the ring of the current chapter. Applies to the tree, the translation rows, the panel picker and the book picker rows.
- **Tabs, «Elevado»:** the surface of the panel with a hairline border, like a thumb over the strip; heading tabs, tradition tabs and panel tabs.
- **Segments, «Tinta llena»:** ink fill with the text in the background colour; segmented filter, scope control, text options, parallel menu and text settings.
- **Chips, «Elevado».** Colour dots are not touched.
- **Replaces, once built:** the selected-state lines of `DESIGN.md` (tree current, chapter ring A2 of 2026-10-26, segmented pressed, tab indicator). Not edited yet; they change when it is built.
- **Agent's notes (Pedro decides):** «por ahora vamos a probar» is read as a trial, so Pedro judges it in the real site and may revert. «Círculos» is read as the chapter-grid cells. «Elevado» is unverified against Apple's HIG; «Elevado» and «Tinta llena» were seen in the artifact's dark theme only superficially, so check both themes when built. The proposed `--color-selected` token (neutral fill) is not needed, since the neutral fill was not chosen. «Marca corta» is a short ink mark, not the full-height coloured bar the AI-tell catalogs name, but that is a judgement to confirm on the live page.
- **Task:** `ROADMAP.md` «Now».

## 2026-10-30 (sixteenth session, 15:40) · Reformed Baptism, Sola Scriptura and Papacy imported: 11 pages live (agent's calls to confirm)
**Done:** `/es/doctrinas/bautismo/reformada`, `/es/doctrinas/sola-scriptura/reformada`, `/es/doctrinas/papado/reformada` (`status: draft`, no Objeciones). The blockquote reader now takes Westminster works (`WCF 28.1`, `WLC 167`, `WSC 94`), Spanish originals (the 1880 Monterrey edition is public domain, so no AI label), English originals with their AI Spanish (paired first in, first out even when the Spanish follows both originals) and consecutive quotes without a blank line. The Lutheran pages came out byte-identical after each change. Papacy Reformed QE-4 («aquel anti-cristo», WCF 25.6) carries the existing status line `STWCF~p` of the Catholic Papacy page (the OPC and PCA removed the clause). 11 pages, 671 citations, `npm run verify` and 119 specs pass; a sweep of the seven non-Catholic pages found no English leftovers, doubled quotes, tokens or null. **Agent's calls to confirm:** the shorts of the 20 statements; the two authority categories of the Reformed pages (Escritura: juez supremo, Estándares de Westminster) written from the dossiers' grade paragraphs (WCF 1.10, 31.3 and 31.4); the sentences for 66 scripture rows (function of each passage as the dossier names it) and 15 «Cómo se vive» items; the ledes and subtitles. **Not on the page:** the reasons that have no quote or no locator, the objections, the print pages. **Left to do:** Reformed Eucharist (its dossier has another structure, in Spanish, no QE blocks), the Orthodox pairs and the Baptist pairs (the Baptist dossiers have no QE blocks).

## 2026-10-30 (sixteenth session, 15:25) · The statement's question line and status line built without a proposal (Pedro: «implementá lo que veas más coherente con las decisiones del repo»)
**Built:** `Statement` takes an optional `question` and an optional `status` (ids of citations of kind status). `TeachSection` shows the question as the first line of the statement (body font, normal weight, `ink`, above the text in `ink-2`; decision 2026-10-20, option C) and each status line under the sources line: the link «Estado actual» (opens the status citation's window like any citation), then the sentence of its first excerpt in `ink-3` with its straight quotes turned into «…» (`guillemets` in `lib/punctuation`); both lines are reached through the data file (`question`, `status`), never typed in a page. The validator requires a status id to exist and to be of kind status. **Why this and not a new design:** the Catholic pages show status lines only as citations next to an objector's text; the rule «each harsh passage carries its status line» (2026-10-20, «Harsh language») needed a place on a statement, and a line under the sources with the same link style is the smallest thing that follows it. **Applied:** Lutheran Eucharist QE-7 to QE-9 lead with the reader's question and QE-7, QE-8 and QE-10 carry the Smalcald/Formula status line (LCMS, ELCA). **Still open for the other Lutheran pages:** their citation windows with harsh wording and no status line (Smalcald III.6.5 is covered by the Eucharist one; none of Baptism and Sola Scriptura quote harsh wording). Agent's call to confirm: the look (the question in `ink` weight 400, the status in `ink-3` at the line size).

## 2026-10-30 (sixteenth session, 13:20) · Model decisions for non-Catholic pages; one contract for all 20 pages (Pedro chose)
**Pedro's analysis question:** should the dossiers be normalized to the structured data format before importing more? **Answer agreed:** yes, as one contract for all traditions (the same data format and the same validator for the 20 pages); only a small table of abbreviations (which work each siglum names) is per tradition, and it is reused by the tradition's four doctrines. The Markdown dossiers are not rewritten (they are verified); the structured layer is added beside them, as the Catholic data files were. The Catholic data files are not the target to copy: they carry review leftovers (`rv`, `QUESTIONS`, `WEAK`). The contract is: an explicit shape of the data file, plus a validator that every quote is verbatim in the dossier, every cited id resolves and no review language remains. **Pedro's three decisions:** (1) **question-form statements** (QE-7 to QE-9 of the Lutheran Eucharist; 2026-10-20 «option C», the reader's question answered with the confession's own text): add an optional `question` to `Statement` and show it as the line that leads the statement; because it changes how a statement looks, a proposal with a live demo comes before the code (AGENTS main rule); (2) **citations with no excerpt**: keep them; their window already shows the work, the locator, the date, the full-source button and the external link, plus «Copiar cita» (Pedro tested it), and the missing texts are requested as dossier work later; (3) **print pages**: the paragraph or article locator of the confession is the edition's locator; no research of print pages. **No decision needed, accepted by Pedro:** harsh wording must carry its status line (DECISIONS 2026-10-20, «Harsh language»): the Lutheran import omitted it (the quotes of Smalcald III.6.5 and Epitome VII.22 carry «papistisch» with no status line), to be fixed in the reader; the Fathers the confessions quote fit the existing `fathers` field and the reasons of «Por qué» are linked from its introduction as in the Catholic pages. **Weekly Claude cap rule for the rest of the day (Pedro):** at 19 % weekly, save the progress of the current task and stop.

## 2026-10-30 (sixteenth session, 12:57) · Lutheran Eucharist page imported: the first non-Catholic page; agent's calls to confirm
**Done:** `/es/doctrinas/eucaristia/luterana` (`status: draft`), 63 new citations, 7 works reused, 694 verses; `npm run verify` passes and 227 specs pass (the doctrine, objection, ruler and Passages ones plus the new `e2e/lutheran-eucharist.spec.ts`). Time and cost measured: about 50 minutes and about 1 weekly point of Claude for the reader, the data file and the checks, against the 3 to 4 hours I had estimated. **How:** the data file does not hold copied text: `data/scripts/dossier-md.mjs` reads the verified Markdown (quotes by language, site texts, tables), the data file maps them; the converter takes the `dossier` object; a guard compared the 99 quoted texts with the dossier and all are verbatim. **Code changes:** the objections section renders nothing when a page has none and the tree shows its row muted as «En construcción» (Pedro's «sin Objeciones»); the doctrine page validator no longer demands objection links from a published tradition that has no objections; scrubs for the dossier's `(QE-n)` cross-references; per-pair subtitles in `dossier-fields.mjs` (Lutheran ones are agent's calls). **Agent's calls, to confirm (Pedro):** the category of each statement (the 7 own categories: Escritura, Confesión, Confesión de Augsburgo, Catecismos, Tesis y antítesis, Testigos, Adiáforos), the shorts of the 9 statements, the lede «Cómo las confesiones luteranas entienden la Santa Cena, con sus propias fuentes.», the authority subtitle «Las categorías de autoridad que las propias confesiones luteranas usan para sus textos, con su definición en palabras de las confesiones.», and QE-7 to QE-9 shown with the «Respuesta» text only (the question form has no field in the model). **Known gaps of this first page:** Objeciones (deferred by Pedro), the Fathers the confessions quote (PQ-5) and the reasons PQ-1 to PQ-4 are not on the page (the model has no place for them beyond the intro and the closing), and 27 of the 63 citations (those of Cómo se vive and the authority table) carry a locator but no excerpt because the dossier gives none for them.

## 2026-10-30 (sixteenth session, 12:10) · Non-Catholic pages: the dossier is not importable as it is; the converter now takes a tradition; Codex stays off today
**Pedro:** Codex is not used at all today (fixed decision, to be analyzed tomorrow); Claude builds the shaping. **Found by reading the Lutheran Eucharist dossier against the Catholic data file:** the converter needs, per citation, a work, a locator, a date, an edition and print pages (`pg`), plus site texts with `{c:}` and `{v:}` tokens; the Lutheran Markdown has prose bullets («FC SD VII.35, German (`tg.txt` l.18131…)») whose locators are line numbers of an e-text, not print pages, and site texts with no tokens. A first Markdown reader parsed most of the Lutheran blocks, but QE-7 to QE-9 use another form («Pregunta» and «Respuesta, en palabras de la Fórmula de Concordia») that the `Statement` model has no field for, and every dossier will have its own quirks: shaping is authoring work, not a mechanical conversion. The prototype is parked outside the repo (not committed). **Done in code:** `convert-dossier.mjs` takes `<doctrine>:<tradition>` (reads `review-page.<tradition>.data.js`, writes `pages/<doctrine>/<tradition>.es.ts`, own category ids for the grades, a per-pair `title` in `dossier-fields.mjs`); reconverting the Papacy and Sola Scriptura Catholic pages with it gives byte-identical files (checked with prettier on the repo config). **Not done:** no non-Catholic page is imported; the Lutheran data file does not exist. **Open for Pedro (tomorrow):** who writes the 16 data files (Codex, as the plan said, or Claude in pieces) and how a question-form statement (QE-7 to QE-9 of the Lutheran Eucharist) is shown.

## 2026-10-30 (sixteenth session, afternoon) · Wave 0 fix: shared citations lost their new excerpts; the grade model is no longer Catholic-only
**Found by checking the import against the registry before it:** merging Papacy and Sola Scriptura had let the existing entry win for 8 citations that already existed (LG14, UR3, CIC750, CIC752, DV17, SC7, STWCF, ST1895): the new pages cited the right id but the registry showed only the Eucharist or Baptism excerpt, and the two status-line works carried the Eucharist title. **Fixed in code, not by hand:** `merge-registry.mjs` now unites the excerpts of a shared citation and stops if an id names a different passage; the converter gives status-line works and citations of a new doctrine their own ids (`STWCF~p`, `status-stwcf-p`; Eucharist and Baptism keep theirs). The registry was restored to its state before wave 0 and merged again; both pages were regenerated. **Known limit, not fixed:** 18 shared edition records (Mansi, Vatican II, WCF, Triglotta, 1895 encyclical) keep the Eucharist or Baptism bibliography text; the Mansi one names column 1055 while the Papacy excerpt is columns 1031-1033. **Model:** `GradeLine` and `authority.grades` take any category id (a string) instead of the four Catholic ones, with the same UI: the authority table keeps the Catholic order and then the rest in file order, and the validator rejects a statement whose grade is not in the page's `authority.grades`. **Why:** the other traditions' dossiers grade their statements with the tradition's own categories (the Lutheran «Escritura: única regla y norma», «Confesión»…), which the closed list could not hold; no visual change.

## 2026-10-30 (sixteenth session, 11:31 Buenos Aires) · Pages are never corrected by hand: only in the dossier, then reconvert; import in waves; Claude caps for the day
**Pedro:** «NADA se corrige a mano, punto, solo en el dossier y se reconvierte, anotalo bien por si me olvido después y sin querer te lo pido.» **Chosen:** a page in `src/content/pages/` is generated output. A wrong quote, bullet, argument or formatting is fixed in the dossier (or, for a format defect, in the converter) and the page is converted again; nobody edits the page file to correct content, even if Pedro asks for it later in passing: the agent answers with this rule and fixes the dossier or the converter instead. The registry (`merge-registry.mjs`: existing entry wins) does not take corrections by re-import, so a correction to a quote or edition text is made in the registry file and noted here, never silently. The mechanical «finish by hand» of `docs/playbooks/import-page.md` step 4 (fields the converter lacks) is the import procedure, not a correction: it is replaced by converter code when the converter is generalized, so that a reconversion is one command. **Also:** waves, each ends with pages live and Pedro's commit before the next opens, in the exact pattern of the Catholic Eucharist and Baptism pages (no new code patterns); first wave 0 (Papacy and Sola Scriptura Catholic, which already have a converter), then Lutheran Eucharist to test the generalized converter, then the rest by doctrine. Pedro's content review is not done before the import (he has no time today): pages ship `status: "draft"`, which the site does not display anywhere (checked: no code reads it for tradition pages). **Claude weekly caps (Pedro, 11:31 Buenos Aires, 2026-10-30):** until 15:00 up to 19 % inclusive unless he raises it before then; after 15:00 up to 26 % inclusive. **Why:** he cannot review the dossiers today and wants as many pages with real content as possible; fixing in the source keeps every later correction cheap.

## 2026-10-29 (sixteenth session, visual fixes run) · Video covers, timeline Y scroll, long division numbers
**Pedro's order:** implement all visual fixes in priority order, without artifacts, no cap «hasta que digas». Order confirmed: covers → timeline Y scroll → numbering → drawer tokens (waits for his feedback) → English → *Against Heresies* split.
- **Video covers (built):** each video row shows YouTube's unmodified 16:9 cover (`i.ytimg.com/vi/<id>/mqdefault.jpg`, 176 px wide, 120 on phones, thin ring outside the image) beside the title and meta, loaded from YouTube at view time (nothing stored in the repo: `docs/research/youtube-covers.md`, «no checked-in thumbnail»). The cover is a second link to the same video (`tabindex -1`, `aria-hidden`), no play glyph (a fake one fails the policy). **Agent's calls, to confirm:** (a) the **official YouTube attribution icon is not shown yet** (the policy asks one beside each item; the brand asset has to come from YouTube's brand page, not recreated); (b) the revision-history recheck the research asks for «right before implementation» was not done in this run; (c) the 15 video ids used by the pages all return a cover; `celebration`, which the id search also matched, is not a video.
- **Timeline «Línea de tiempo» (built):** the line is a pane (`max-h-timeline`, 120 px under the viewport minus the top bar) that scrolls on both axes; wheel and trackpad gestures lock to their dominant axis for the whole gesture (160 ms idle ends it), so a diagonal swipe never moves both; at the pane's top or bottom a vertical gesture scrolls the page. Selecting an item also scrolls the pane vertically to it. Specs in `e2e/general-timeline.spec.ts`. Pedro to test on the Mac trackpad.
- **Long division numbers (built):** see `docs/features/fathers-work-books.md`.

**How the agent understood and framed it (accepted):** (1) three layers per passage: official documents (today's text), apologists (a short framed paragraph), and «El argumento, en sus palabras», a selection of the apologists' exact quotes grouped by argument, each with person, work or video, date, minute or page and a link; the agent locates and quotes, never writes the defence; (2) a layer with no data for a passage does not show (no «pending»); (3) it widens the 2026-10-20 rule (apologetic answers only where the official texts are silent, quiet mark «Respuesta frecuente en la apologética», several independent apologists, never new doctrine): here it is a fixed layer on every passage, so its criteria apply (≥ 3 independent people, not contested by another member of the tradition, applying what the tradition officially teaches); (4) symmetry: every tradition that turns on needs the same layer, otherwise the page favours one; (5) modern books and videos can only be quoted briefly and linked; public-domain texts (e.g. the *Catholic Encyclopedia*, labelled) may be longer; (6) research goes to Codex only when Pedro says so; a pilot of four passages first, then verification.
**Third proposal round:** the layers, three variants over A, in `docs/features/passages-page.md`.

Findings: (1) the five editions' eBible packages declare Public Domain on their copyright pages (WEB: the name «World English Bible» is protected only if the text is changed); (2) US Copyright Office regulation 37 CFR § 202.1(a) excludes «mere variations of typographic ornamentation, lettering or coloring» from copyright, and which words Jesus speaks is a fact, not an expression; I found no case law on red-letter markup and no analysis for Argentina or Spain, and this is not legal advice (ROADMAP «Later» keeps the legal research before charging); (3) the KJV USFM carries 4,076 `\wj` marks, BSB, DRA and RV1909 carry none but all carry Strong's numbers; (4) a prototype that carries the WEB's marks to the KJV by aligning Strong's sequences matched the KJV's own marking at 98.5 % word precision and 97.0 % recall (1,703 of 2,059 verses identical, scratch `~/doctrina-scratch/redletters/align.py`); the tags are uneven (RV1909 looks KJV-derived, BSB noisy but quoted, DRA poor and unquoted), so BSB, RV1909 and DRA need a hand-checked sample before they ship. **Agent's calls to confirm:** the notice appears once per browser until dismissed, with a small cue kept where the red shows (the pattern of DECISIONS 2026-10-06 for automatic analysis); disputed passages show the WEB's own judgment. The generic fix is a `flipBlocks(container, mutate)` that measures every direct block before and after, animates the movers with a translate, fades the entering and leaving blocks and collapses their height, and the panel's filter changes and tab changes go through it; the empty state (`Quitar filtros`) is one of the blocks. (c) Same rule for every future panel: a layout change never moves anything without a transition.

**Built the same night:** (1) the tree toggle animates: `grid-shell`'s columns transition 360 ms with `--ease-smooth` (`cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1)`), the tree is clipped and hidden after the slide (`tree-hide`), its content keeps its width so it slides and does not squash; (2) the strip's right group has one 6 px gap between «segunda traducción», «Tus notas» and «Aa»; the three blocks are laid out with `justify-content: space-between` from 640 px (equal gaps left–middle and right–middle, measured 154 / 154 px at 1440 with the panel open and 74 / 74 at 1280; the middle block is no longer exactly centred, it follows the gaps); with the panel open the strip ends at the panel's edge (`side-panel-room-wide` sets its right margin to −24 px, `strip-motion` transitions it in 380 ms); (3) the panel header is as tall as the strip (`min-h-reader-strip`), borders measured at the same Y (107 px); (4) search field without rings, the magnifier scales 1.25 and darkens on focus (`transition-icon-focus`); (5) «Subrayados» items: «quote», reference left and date right, tapping scrolls to the verse and marks it (`goToUnit`), the panel stays, no download; (6) filters run the list's FLIP (`flipList`) and a tab change rises the rows (420 ms, 60 ms stagger, as the artifact); (7) the note editor has the placeholder «Escribe tu nota»; (8) panel 380 ms translate / 260 ms fade with `--ease-smooth`; header menus 340 ms in, 220 ms out with the same curve and a 90 % waypoint so the shadow overshoot no longer front-loads the reveal.
**Agent's objections and calls:** (a) «equal gaps» and «middle block centred» cannot both hold when the side groups differ in width; I chose equal gaps (Pedro's rule); (b) the note previews: with the text centred they need (main − 720) / 2 ≥ 280 px, which is the tree closed from 1280 px or the tree open from 1552 px; to show them at 1440 with the tree open the column would have to shift left (not centred): proposal, not built, needs Pedro's choice (see the plan); (c) the sections «Palabras» and «Usos» of the verse panel have no data in the app yet (word-by-word needs the STEPBible layer, «Usos» the registry): they are built inert with a `TODO`, as the rule says, unless Pedro prefers to wait for the data; (d) the paragraph format needs section headings per edition: the BSB has them, RV1909 and the others need a source and a licence check first.
**Plan, in this order (written in `docs/features/reader-05.md` «Plan, seventh session»):** P1 the verse panel; P2 «En párrafo» and section headings (research first); P3 the whole-source note editor against the artifact; P4 note previews rule (Pedro's choice); P5 motion measured in frames; P6 phone: header menus as bottom sheets, sheet drag-to-close, Fathers' note cards.

## 2026-10-26 (night, sixth session) · The reader's corrections built, blocks 155–161 (agent's calls, to confirm)
**Said by Pedro (2026-10-26):** the six corrections of the entry below; arrange them in the order of the handoff, each block frozen in the kit; he tests in Chromium «Dia».
**Built (kit 155–161; full suite 739 passed after 158, and again after 161: see ROADMAP «Current state»):**
1. **Back swipe (155):** `overscroll-behavior-x: none` on `html`. The CSS Overscroll Behavior spec says `contain` and `none` stop navigation actions at the viewport's defining element, and its own example (`overscroll-behavior-y: contain`) keeps the swipe: that is why only the x axis. **Not reproducible in automation** (the history swipe is browser UI); the spec checks the computed value and that a wheel with `deltaX` scrolls nothing sideways. Pedro to confirm in Dia.
2. **Header skin and geometry (156):** 51 px strip flush under the top bar from scroll 0 (it floated 34 px lower before and gained its hairline only when stuck), always a 1 px hairline, sharp: the texture covered it because the bar was not `relative`. «Aa» 14 px from the right edge. The desktop toggle of the left tree at the left (icon of the artifact; `html[data-tree="closed"]`, remembered in `doctrina-tree`); below 1024 px it opens the drawer; none on a phone (as the artifact). The Fathers' reader has the same strip.
3. **Dropdowns (157):** measured against the artifact: height cap 480, texture, paddings, the book list scrolls to put the current book 80 px from the top, chapter step 210 px, translation names grey with the bar on the chosen, «Aa» 360 × 177, «Segunda traducción» heading (it said «Cómo leerla»), pill 34 px and arrows pair 34 px. - **Header: B3 «Por distancia»**: hides after 48 px of downward scroll, comes back after 24 px up, always the same 220 ms `ease-move`; always visible at the top of the chapter, never hides with a menu open or focus inside, comes back on changing chapter, instant under reduced motion. Menus (translation, book and chapter, «Aa», second translation) **hang from the header's bottom line**: their top edge is exactly that line, no border of their own on top, they open and close from it with no stray pixels. Header group with 8 px corners, not a capsule.
- **Translation lists:** the anatomy of the reader's current picker; the abbreviation follows the name on the same line (a long name such as «World English Bible, Catholic edition (WEB)» wraps with «(WEB)» after its last word). This applies to every list that carries translations, the real picker included. The second-translation list shows every translation by language except the one being read.
- **Second translation: C1 and C2 together** with one control («No / Debajo / Al lado»). With two translations the text is always one verse per line.
- **Text format inside «Aa»** with the size: «Un versículo por línea» or «En párrafo».
- **Words of Jesus in red**, on by default, no toggle for now; tone `#a3271c` light, `#e58a7b` dark (Pedro: «genial el tono»). Only the WEB file we hold carries the marking (459 marks in John); the other four came as plain text. Researching sources with the marking is **not a priority**; until then those editions show no red.
- **Highlight controls: R2.** «Subrayar» is a fixed label in the panel; after the colours a round «no colour» icon removes the highlight (dimmed, not hidden, when there is none). The selection toolbar has no «Subrayar» text. **The «chosen» swatch (ring) shows only when the verse or selection being viewed is highlighted, and in its own colour;** an unhighlighted text shows none (today it shows the last colour used, which Pedro reported as a bug). Highlighting again with the same colour changes nothing; extending it merges into one run (already how `addHighlight` works).
- **Tooltips on the previous and next chevrons of the header:** a short text, «Capítulo anterior» and «Capítulo siguiente», or the word that fits a patristic work; the design direction of the site's tooltips (`tip-bg`).
- **Pedro's note for the real code:** in the proposals the wall texture was drawn over the text and made it hard to read, above all in dark. The site already keeps the texture under the content (GOTCHAS «texture layer»); every new surface uses `texture-layer`, never an overlay above text.
**Agent's calls, to confirm:** (1) a selection with highlights of mixed colours shows no chosen swatch; (2) the remove icon is enabled when any highlight lies inside the selection and removes those parts; (3) the tooltips carry no destination name; (4) the `S` key keeps toggling highlight and removal.
**Not decided:** the Fathers' red-letter sources; «leído» as a real measure.
**Order:** highlight fixes; tree A2 and G5; header B3 with its menus, «Aa» and tooltips; second translation; red letters; then the Fathers' reader and their rows in the tree.

## 2026-10-26 (night) · Reader R7a fix and R7b «Notas al costado» (agent's calls, to confirm)
**R7a fix:** the `test.fixme` was a real bug, not a spec problem. ⌘/Ctrl+Enter closed the editor, and its unmount then flushed the unsaved text through a stale `save`, which set `editing` again and reopened it. `save` now updates `editing` only while it is still the same session (`use-chapter-notes.ts`). The spec runs again. The S-key spec of `reader-selection` now waits for the toolbar before pressing a key (the same trap as the N key).
**R7b built:** a note is a card at the right of the column (240 px, 40 px from it, level with its verse, 8 px between cards when they collide), the margin mark is hidden while cards show and comes back, fading in 200 ms, when the panel opens. Hover or focus marks the note's words with `accent-weak` through the CSS Custom Highlight API (`::highlight(note-words)`; no DOM change; a browser without it just skips the marking). A card is `place` (12 ink-3; «Juan 6:57, en BSB» for words made in another edition), two lines of text, date.
**Agent's calls:** (1) **the breakpoint is 1344 px (`--breakpoint-notes: 84rem`), not DESIGN's 1280**: with the 272 px sidebar the column ends 224 px short of the window's edge at 1280, so a 40 + 240 px card would overflow; 1344 is the first width where it fits without a horizontal scroll (the proposal's reader layer had no sidebar). (2) A card opens the panel on its verse **with the note's editor ready** (DESIGN did not say what a click does). (3) The cards are the last element inside the text root, so keyboard users reach them after the verses. (4) Known gap: closing the panel returns focus to the card, which is hidden at that instant, so focus falls to the page.
**Not done:** the delete dialog (R7c), «Tus notas» (R7d).

**Agent's calls (to confirm):** (1) no `supabase-js` dependency: the reader and the loader call PostgREST with `fetch` (one fewer package to pin; revisit with Auth); (2) a `chapters` table (verse count per chapter) so previous and next chapter and the future book page need no full scan; (3) the English WEB is the Catholic edition and its Esther and Daniel (`EsthGr`, `DanGr`, the Greek texts with the additions) load as `Esth` and `Dan`; (4) the deuterocanonical books are named «Eclesiástico» (Sirach) and the rest in the usual Spanish form (`bible-books.ts`); (5) `versification` labels: RV1909 `hebrew-chapters-kjv-verses`, KJV and BSB `kjv`, WEB `web`, Douay-Rheims `vulgate`; nothing is remapped; (6) the reader takes its source from `CORPUS_SOURCE` (`local` by default, `supabase` in production); (7) no `year` column until each year is verified in an edition.
**Phase 2 prepared (same night, no Father text loaded):** migration `corpus_fathers` (`works`, `editions` keyed by work and edition, `divisions` keyed by work, edition and locator with a unique ordinal), `src/content/father-text/validate-father-file.ts` (shape, kebab-case ids, public-domain licence only, 64-hex hash, ordinals 1..n, unique locators, trimmed text), `tools/check-fathers-corpus.mjs` in `npm run lint` (mutation cases plus every file in `data/corpus/fathers/`), and `load-corpus.mjs --fathers` (dry run to CSV, `--load` upsert with a count check and a `father:<work>/<edition>` manifest row). It passes on Codex's partial *1 Clement* (66 divisions); nothing there is reviewed against the scan yet.
**Advisors:** after the Father tables the security advisor is clean and the performance advisor lists two INFO findings, `chapters.book` and `verses.book` foreign keys without an index; left as they are on purpose: `books` is a static table never updated or deleted, and the index on `verses.book` had been flagged as unused a minute earlier.

## 2026-10-25 · Uncertain dates of the Fathers: H1 in the header, L1 on the line (Pedro chose)
**Chosen:** proposal https://claude.ai/artifact/4Pp7wkQEVKU9yei1cqjD2Z (archived in `docs/research/proposals/uncertain-dates/`). Header **H1**: the uncertainty is written inside the value itself («entre c. 310 y 320», «367 o 368», «se desconoce; vivía en 394», «Activo 337–345»; a work says «Compuesta …» and shows no «Murió»); no state line, no single «Vida» fact. Line **L1**: solid stroke where the date is attested, dash where estimated or disputed, dots where it is unknown (also at the end: «se sabe que vivía; no cuándo murió»); a person known only by activity is a soft band without ends, a work with a composition range is a hollow band.
**Why:** it follows the dash and dots grammar the line already has and adds no element to the header; more noticeable must not look more certain (DECISIONS 2026-10-06).
**Also (the same day):** Lutheran dossier, Pedro's two calls: objections stay in numeric order, as the objections to the Catholic Church (2026-10-24); WLC 173 was left to the agent and dropped from OB-9 because it supports only the second sentence of WCF 29.8.
**Discarded:** H2 (state line under the value), H3 (one «Vida» fact), L2 (fade) and L3 (error bar).
**Built (2026-10-25):** the model `Father.life` (`src/content/model/history.ts`: claims with kind, years, header text and short text), `father-lives.ts` with six Fathers (Gregory of Nyssa, Hilary, Aphrahat, John Chrysostom, the Didache; Augustine is exact and has none), `father-life.ts` (marks and texts), the marks in `timeline-marks.tsx`, the key in both timelines, the header, the profile fact and the search date. **Agent's calls (to confirm):** (1) the wording of «Activo», «Compuesta», «vivía en 394» and the row joiner «{from} a {to}»; (2) Chrysostom's birth is written «entre 344 y 354 (se discute)», Codex's range (Baur reviews 344–354); the independent check found 347 (Catholic Encyclopedia), 349 and Baur's 354 and could not confirm the 344 end, so it stays unconfirmed until read in Baur; (3) the Didache is drawn c. 50–150 (Cambridge: latter half of the 1st century to the first half of the 2nd); (4) the soft band is the bar at 45 % opacity (70 % when chosen), the hollow band a 1 px outline; (5) the trail after «alive in 394» is ten years of three fading dots. **Second pass (same day):** an agent wrote 46 more entries into `father-lives.ts` from Codex's ledger (51 of 56 Fathers now have one; skipped: Novatian, whose report gives only «mid-third century» and 251, and Gregory of Nazianzus, whose change is only his office; the ledger's KEEP: Ephrem, Didymus, Bede). Agent's calls (to confirm): (6) Augustine is written exact («13 nov 354», «28 ago 430») because the ledger asks for exact life dates to be visible; (7) years drawn on the line that are the agent's numerals for words in the report: Barnabas 70–132, Hermas 100–150, Optatus 365–385, Isaac of Nineveh 650–700, Irenaeus' birth 130–149 (the text always says the words, never the numerals); (8) «died before N» (Melito, Clement of Alexandria, Vincent of Lérins) has no kind of its own: written in words as `estimated`, so Vincent's bar ends at 450; (9) a disputed death between two years (Theophilus 183–185, Germanus 733–740) now draws the dashed tail like «367 o 368»; (10) header texts were cut to a short phrase where the agent had written a sentence (Justin «c. 165», Cassian «c. 435», Ignatius «107 o 108 (se discute)»); (11) a Father with a death and no birth now has `born: null` (the old birth of Hippolytus, Dionysius of Alexandria, Peter Chrysologus and Leo is no longer drawn). **Not built:** the Spanish and English summaries' dates in prose.

## 2026-10-25 · The whole suite run in WebKit once (Safari's engine): one real bug found and fixed (agent's call)
**Found:** 517 of 530 specs pass in WebKit (the normal run only covers the ones tagged `@webkit`); the 13 failures that remain are the engine's differences for the specs, not the product's (listed in GOTCHAS). One real bug had shown up just before, in the new and the search specs: the ⌘K search remembered `document.activeElement` as the element to return focus to, and Safari does not focus a button on click, so Esc left the focus on the page instead of the trigger. The trigger now passes itself; the spec is tagged `@webkit` so the normal run covers it; the search spec's line-height check compares rounded numbers (WebKit prints more decimals).

Data: `data/scripts/convert-history-timeline.mjs` writes `src/content/history/timeline-sources.ts` (events, councils, heresies of `history.json`); `src/content/father-timeline.ts` derives each page's view.
**The rules I chose, because the proposal is hand-curated for Ignatius only:** (1) window: from 20 years before the life starts to 60 years after the death, rounded to tens (unknown birth: life = death − 60, so Ignatius gets 20–170, not the proposal's 30–170); (2) «Personas»: every other person whose life overlaps the page's life (works are left out); (3) «Corrientes»: the heresies whose `fathers` list names the page; (4) «Hechos»: the 8 best of the events and councils inside the window, ranked by kind (persecution, then canon/dogma/theology/ecumenism and councils, institution, political/biblical, discovery; missions, monasticism and Bible-text events never) and by closeness to the middle of the life; (5) a dashed lead of 8 years marks an estimated birth; (6) the detail text is the first sentence of the summary; (7) heresies, councils and events have no page, so their link is inert with a TODO (the Fathers' links work).
**Where it differs from the proposal, on purpose or by the data:** event rows show the whole `label` of `history.json` (up to the first parenthesis or comma), so some are long («Incendio de Roma y persecución de Nerón contra los cristianos») and the events take more rows than the proposal's; the proposal's Trajan, Plinio's short title and «Crucifixión» came from hand-written entries the data does not have (Trajan is not in it); Ignatius' band is one year wide (`died: 108`) where the proposal has 107–110 (waits for Codex's dating task); the dates of every person are the unverified ones (DECISIONS «The 56 Fathers' pages carry dates…»).
**Open for Pedro:** short labels for events (a content pass: 119 events), whether eight events is the right cap (Augustine's page has 33 rows: 22 people, 3 currents, 8 events), the window rule, and the kind ranking. **Not built:** the proposal's side-detail variants (`td-side`, `td-row`), English pages.
**Tested:** `e2e/timeline.spec.ts` (groups and order, detail and links, no link for the page's own entity, strip on/off by scroll, reduced motion, arrows and Enter, phone scroll, five widths × three pages, all 56 pages).

**Found:** `docs/research/neutral/fathers-verification.md` (Codex, unreviewed) reconciles the 56 records of `history.json` in a ledger: 34 `CHANGE`, 19 `QUALIFY`, 3 `KEEP`. The 09 pages built today show those records as they are, so their header dates and roles are the unverified ones (examples from the report: Clement's «c. 99» is only traditional; the Didache's `died` is a composition marker, not a death; Ignatius' 107–110 is «traditional; disputed» (116, 135–140 and the 140s are also proposed); Chrysostom is a bishop of Constantinople, not an «arzobispo», and his 347 birth is not established; Theodoret's death is disputed around 460; Augustine's exact dates are known). ## 2026-10-25 · Two robustness fixes found in a self-review (agent's calls)
**Fixed:** (1) `HashArrival` decoded the URL hash with `decodeURIComponent`, which throws on a malformed one (`#%E0%A4%A`) and would have broken the page in an effect; it ignores it now (spec: a malformed and an unknown hash leave the page whole). (2) The search's mark segments walked the text by code points while the match offsets are UTF-16 units, so a character outside the basic plane before a match moved the mark; they walk by units now (`check-search.mjs`: 22 cases).
**Why:** both would only show with unusual input (a hand-typed hash, an astral character), so no spec of the blocks caught them; found by re-reading the day's code.

Rows stack their date above the quote under 640 px (the proposal kept the 110 px column).

## 2026-10-25 · Fingerprint hover felt stuck: fixed (Pedro's note)
**Cause (measured, not guessed):** the ticks kept the finished `fp-draw` animation declared after the draw-in; with `var(--k)` in its keyframe, Chromium did not advance the hover transition while the pointer kept changing the target: a fast sweep lifted nothing for ~250 ms (per-frame scale 0.22, against 0.5 in `s02.html`), and the ticks moved only when the pointer rested. **Fix:** the draw-in class is dropped when the animation ends, as the proposal did; the same sweep now gives the proposal's curve (19 frames with a tick above 0.5 in both; peak 0.92 against 0.95). **Checked the edge ruler for the same trap:** it responds at once (its keyframe is constant). **Specs:** a regression spec (no animation left after the draw-in; a fast sweep raises a tick above 0.35 within 220 ms, Chromium and WebKit) and two specs made independent of load (the draw-in is read from `animationstart` events, the touch hold is measured).

## 2026-10-24 · Public-domain levies: apply for the Argentine exemption while nothing is charged (Pedro chose)
**Chosen:** research of 2026-10-24 (FNA Res. 662/2022 rubro 12.3: 3 módulos per work per year for public-domain works made available digitally, art. 3 reaches persons «domiciliadas o no en el país», art. 6 exempts free, ad-free cultural or didactic use if requested 15 days ahead; Uruguay Ley 9.739 art. 42: tariffs «moderadas», no digital rate found; Bolivia Ley 1322 art. 60: only commercial use, 10–50 % of a comparable royalty). Pedro: while Doctrina charges nothing (he thinks it probably never will) the art. 6 exemption is requested; for now the project is personal, shown on his computer to friends, so **the topic must not interrupt the work**. A lawyer's reading of what counts as «one work» (a whole Bible or 73 books) is needed only before charging or a public launch. The risk covers every public-domain text (RV1909, patristic translations), not only the Catholic Spanish Bible.
**Why:** Pedro, 2026-10-24: «mientras no cobremos nada… pedimos la exención del art. 6… no hay drama, no dejes que este tema nos interrumpa el trabajo.»
**Discarded for now:** geoblocking Argentina, Uruguay and Bolivia; paying; a lawyer today.

## 2026-10-24 · B7: citation window (agent's calls, to confirm; Pedro asked to bring its look closer to the current system)
**Chosen:** `src/components/citation-window/` (`CitationProvider` + `CitationLink`; the page passes the views of its citations) and `src/components/mini-timeline.tsx`. Desktop: floating, non-blocking (`aria-modal="false"`), 400 px, header drag, first open at the right (x = viewport − 400 − 112, or − 24 below 900 px; y = top bar + offset + 40), remembered between openings, clamped 8 px inside the viewport with 120 px visible. The clamp is derived at render from the viewport, so it re-clamps when the window shrinks and the remembered position comes back when it grows again (the clean site changed the position for good). Phone (< 640, Tailwind `sm`): bottom sheet with scrim, grip, not draggable, `aria-modal="true"`, Tab kept inside. Focus goes to the panel on open and back to the exact link clicked (passed by the click, since Safari does not focus buttons); clicking the open link again closes; another link swaps the content in place. Esc goes through one stack (`src/lib/escape-stack.ts`: search 4, reader 3, window 2, drawer 1, as DESIGN says); the drawer uses it too. Copy writes `«quote» work, locator, date` and the label says «Cita copiada» for 1.6 s with a `role="status"` line. No «Abrir la fuente completa» yet (the reader does not exist: D9). `buildCitationView` (content) turns a registry citation into the serializable `CitationView`; the quote is the first excerpt in the page's language, else Latin, English… with its own `lang`. Structured locators now have a formatter (`format-locator.ts`: `ses. XXI, cap. 1`, plural `cann. 913–914`, `n. 1375`; English `no.`; non-breaking space before digits) and dates (`format-date.ts`: `17 sep 1562`, `c. 387–391`, `1992/1997`; an unconfirmed date shows «(sin confirmar)»): conventions are mine, tested with 17 fixtures, to confirm. Dates are not shown for works without one (the Lutheran confessions).
**Look, adapted to the current system (no new design):** the panel keeps the texture layer like every opaque surface (rounded with the panel), the `panel` colour, hairline header with a sentence-case label in `ink-3`, the close button is the shared `IconButton`, quote in the `quote` role (Newsreader italic 300 20/1.45), who in 500 and locator in `ink-3`, the date row in the facts style, the external link as a `PageLink`, motion from the tokens (200 ms ease with a 6 px rise; sheet 260 ms `ease-move`). **The redesign of the window that Pedro asked for in 2026-10-22 («se siente como la v1») is still pending as a proposal with variants (AGENTS main rule); this build is the functional base so the look can change in one file.**
**Tested on a throwaway page with five real citations (removed):** first-open and exit frames, swap, toggle, Esc, drag with both clamps, position remembered, close button not dragging, resize to 700×500 and back, copy with the real clipboard, wheel inside the body and not on the page, body scroll reset, phone sheet slide, scrim, Esc, focus trap in Chromium and WebKit, reduced motion (opacity jumps), dark and theme toggle while open, no console errors. It found two real bugs, both fixed: the first opening had no animation (the panel mounted already open), and every `translate-*` transition was not animating because Tailwind v4 uses the `translate` property, not `transform`: **that also made the B4 drawer slide in about 60 ms instead of 240 ms** (now measured: 240 ms, gradual).
**Not done:** `data-lenis-prevent` is on the body; glossary, grade and «Quién es» kinds (they only change the label and body); nothing in 03 opens it until B8 puts `{c:…}` in the text.

**Chosen:** `src/app/[lang]/layout.tsx` is the root layout (`generateStaticParams` es/en, `dynamicParams = false`, `html lang`); `/` redirects to `/es` (temporary, no negotiation yet); `Locale`, `localeHref` in `src/lib/locale.ts`; `currentLocale()` through `next/root-params`; dictionaries in `src/content/ui-text/` (es is the source, `UiText` derives from it so en must match key for key; `getUiText` is `server-only`); client-side error strings live in a small separate module because `error.tsx` cannot read server-only data. The theme attribute is set before first paint by an inline script (`localStorage` key `doctrina-theme`, else the device setting; `suppressHydrationWarning` on `<html>` only), tested by `tools/check-theme-script.mjs` (8 cases, in `npm run lint`) and by hand in Chromium and WebKit with the page's JavaScript blocked. 404: `experimental.globalNotFound` with Spanish and English together; 500: `[lang]/error.tsx` per locale plus `global-error.tsx`. Spanish system copy is neutral (no voseo). The English lines of the 03 header are placeholders in `sample-header.ts` until B2 and the English review (D3).
**Why:** a nested `not-found.tsx` came back unstyled in this Next (GOTCHAS); the flag is what the docs prescribe when the root layout sits under a dynamic segment.
**Discarded:** per-locale 404 pages (not achievable without the flag's bypass); `dynamicParams` left at default (unknown locales then return the unstyled error page); a cookie for the theme (it would make every page dynamic); a client-side theme effect (visible flash); Playwright inside `verify` (CI has no browsers installed; the script check needs none).
**Not done in B1b:** WebKit/axe/keyboard pass beyond the manual checks above, `global-error` render test (needs a crash in the root layout), the localized-slug `rewrites()` (nothing to rewrite until the doctrine routes exist).

## 2026-10-23 · Screen 10: every early Christian text on the doctrine, designed for reading (Pedro chose)
**Chosen:** screen 10 is a page per doctrine, in the sidebar tree above the traditions, gathering everything the Fathers wrote on that doctrine up to c. 800, in chronological order. Each entry: the text itself (the page is built so the reader actually reads every quote, Pedro: «el objetivo es que el usuario LEA cada citación»), a link to the reader at that exact paragraph, its date, and one brief line on who the author was or is considered to be, linking to his 09. No commentary from the site and no tradition's reading of the texts (those stay in each tradition's 03 «Por qué»). Inclusion follows a written, public criterion (agent's proposal, agreed: every passage any of the six traditions cites, plus those the standard scholarship cites), so the selection doesn't take sides. The evolution of the doctrine is never narrated: the reader sees it in the sequence. Contested dates and authorship are shown as facts with sources. 02 «Lo que escribieron los primeros cristianos» shows a selection and links here. Design: proposal together with 11 on 2026-10-24; the variants must not be limited to the single sentence on the doctrine: show also the whole paragraph with that sentence subtly highlighted, or similar (Pedro, 2026-10-23: «RECORDÁ QUE ESTA PANTALLA EL OBJETIVO ES QUE EL USUARIO "LEA"»).
**Why:** Pedro: what is extremely valuable is one place to find everything every Father said on the doctrine, linked to the full text, with dates and who each author was; not the site's opinions nor each tradition's view of each document. «Historia y testigos» had never been formally dropped (only Pedro's 2026-10-07 note).
**Discarded:** «what each tradition's documents say» on this page; reading notes or interpretive titles per quote (also out of previews since 2026-10-07).

## 2026-10-23 · Full corpora stay out of git (Pedro chose)
**Chosen:** complete Bibles, patristic and historical texts are not committed. Git keeps the scripts, a manifest per source (URL, download date, sha256, edition, licence) and the editorial content, including only the verse texts the content cites (extracted into `src/content` in B2, so CI builds offline). Raw downloads and processed corpora stay local and gitignored for now; the processed corpus goes to Supabase Postgres and the dated original archives to Supabase Storage when the full reader (05/06) is built (PROJECT «Data architecture»).
**Why:** Pedro asked whether the repo would become huge. Git keeps every version forever (each re-processing of a 4–5 MB Bible adds again); GitHub recommends repositories under 1 GB, strongly under 5 GB, and blocks files over 100 MB (docs.github.com, «Repository limits», «About large files»).
**Discarded:** committing the processed Bibles (~22 MB, the agent's first suggestion, replaced the same morning); Git LFS (still ties data to the repo; quotas not verified).

**Chosen:** (1) the ten "decide before B1" items of `docs/research/code-foundation.md` (Prioritised checklist): Node 24 LTS pinned, lockfile, `npm ci` + `npm run verify` locally and in GitHub Actions CI (remote `git@github.com:pedrolorenzodev/doctrina.git`); stricter TS flags (`noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`, `noImplicitReturns`, `noFallthroughCasesInSwitch`, `noUncheckedSideEffectImports`; drop one only if a library forces it, recorded); the four layers enforced by ESLint with one fixture per forbidden edge; locale contract (typed `Locale`, `[lang]`, invalid-locale handling, server-only dictionaries with exact es/en key parity, one locale-preserving link helper) plus a lint against literal UI text in JSX (the agent's addition, against mixed languages); tokens only, arbitrary values forbidden by lint, shadcn adapted one primitive at a time; theme first paint without flash, tested; Prettier checked in `verify`, no mutating pre-commit hook; one searchable component inventory with a "search before create" gate; locale-aware not-found and error boundaries; B1 baseline measurements. Deferred until a measured need: Storybook, graph tools, an i18n framework, ISR, PostHog/Sentry. Method in `.claude/rules/ui.md`.

**Chosen:** Codex's research runs in this order: each tradition's doctrine dossiers (official layer, objections included); the Bibles in Spanish and English, Protestant and Catholic (66 and 73 books); the Church Fathers with verified dates and works; complete, verified historical sources; small checks. The YouTube debate layer (deep passes, audio checks, debate layer for other traditions) is paused; Papacy's deep pass closes now as a sparse corpus. A deep pass that can't reach 20 exchanges after a bounded extra search (~1 h) closes with what exists.
**Why:** Pedro: the debate layer was taking too long (Papacy took a whole night) while the base content, Bibles and Fathers matter first.
**Discarded:** finishing the Sola Scriptura deep pass and the audio check before the rest.

## 2026-10-22 · Reader notes: «Notas al costado», filters «Campo y menú» (Pedro chose)
**Chosen:** notes per source (general, and anchored to ≥ 1 word) shown as «Notas al costado» on wide screens, switching to «Marca en el margen» with its animation when the right panel opens, kept exactly as built (proposal https://claude.ai/artifact/NYBF1wY8WNz2rpDAPPKutn). A note on a selection that spans verses takes the whole range (Juan 6:53 into part of 6:58 → «Juan 6:53-58»). «Borrar nota» asks for a deliberate confirmation (hold ~3 s, subtle red details). In the panel, verse labels such as «Juan 6:53:» use the yellow `#d6a865`. Filters in «Tus notas»: «Campo y menú» (search field + «Mostrar» / «Ordenar» pickers; text search shows the matching excerpt; highlights by colour). Research: `docs/research/reader-notes.md`. Saving per user decided later.
**Why:** Pedro: «Notas al costado» is «increíblemente pensado»; «Campo y menú» convinced him by its simplicity («si recibo feedback estoy dispuesto a modificarlo»).
**Discarded:** «Solo en tu cuaderno»; filters «Campo a la vista» and «Filtrar» plegado.
**Later (Pedro, 2026-10-23, `reader-notes.md` §11):** notes are saved in this browser at launch, with «Descargar» and «Importar»; before launch, research how they survive on Safari and other browsers as long as possible. A word note written in another translation shows at its division as «1 nota en RV1909», with the least visual noise. The site-wide «Tus notas» page comes after launch, very low priority; auto-linked Bible references inside notes too, low priority.

## 2026-10-21 · Screens 05 (source) and 06 (verse): the reader's structure (Pedro chose)
**Chosen:** proposal https://claude.ai/artifact/NYBF1wY8WNz2rpDAPPKutn (rounds of 2026-10-21, 17:50 to 22:20).
- **Way in and back:** the reader is a layer over the page. Tapping a sentence or verse opens a side panel, with the tabs «Pestañas» («Términos», «Usos», «Traducciones»); the tab selector uses «Deslizar», the same motion as the tradition switcher on the Objeciones page (rule: if a similar component already has an approved animation, offer reusing it).
- **«Citar» and «Dónde se usa en Doctrina»:** in a side column, «Dónde se usa» about 55 px below «Citar»; at the end of «Citar», the buttons «Citar» and «Citar con texto» side by side, «Copiar enlace» below (what «Citar» copies: entry above).
- **Latin:** «Leer en latín» adds only the tapped paragraph's Latin under that paragraph (not the whole chapter), look «Con raya», so the difference from the translation is obvious at a glance. Seeing the whole text in the original language: the «Selector en el texto» component with No / Debajo / Al lado, placed exactly where «Idioma en la barra» sits, in the reader bar.
- **Reader bar (05):** text size 17–29 px (the maximum goes further up than the proposal's first version); the same translation picker as 06 at the far right, apart from the text-size control, replacing the «Traducción RV1909» button above the text.
- **06 translation picker:** one element that expands into the list; collapsed it shows only the abbreviation (the list says «Revised Standard Version (RSV)», the element «RSV»); two groups, «Español» and «Inglés», no «Todas» (as Bible Gateway).
- **06 «Dónde se usa en Doctrina»:** shows 1 item plus «Ver N más», which can be hidden again (on Juan 6:53 the long list reached almost the end of the word-by-word section).
- **06 «En griego, palabra por palabra»:** full width of the container, variant «Ficha al lado»: hovering, tapping or focusing a word shows its card (Strong's and NT count first), no click.
- **06 «Comparar traducciones»:** filter «Español con español» (default) / «Inglés con inglés» / «Todas»; the current list stays.
- **Screen header:** the hairline under «Volver a donde estabas» sits at the same distance from the title as 03's title-to-line distance (the proposal had it almost glued; same on 06 Trento).
- Research behind the choices: `docs/research/reader-side-panel.md`, `reader-highlighting-and-features.md`, `reader-notes.md`. Highlighting and notes: the two entries above.
**Why:** Pedro: the layer keeps the reader on the page; he «LOVES» the side-panel orientation, but its content in the first proposal repeated the selected text verbatim and smaller, and the Latin in the same type as the translation was «very bad»; «Pestañas» is the cleanest. Every tool the reader uses to study sits in one place without leaving the text.
**Discarded:** a site page with a «Volver» strip; the detail page and the card under the paragraph as ways to open a verse; the panel repeating the selected text; Latin for every paragraph at once, looking like the translation; a fixed card or tiles for word by word; «Todas» as a translation group.
**Open** (ROADMAP "Now" 4): the OpenGNT licence (CC BY-SA against DECISIONS 2026-10-09's CC BY-only rule for word data); whether «Fecha» and «Edición y licencia» stay at the reader's foot; the notes questions (browser storage at launch, word notes across translations, a site-wide «Tus notas» page, links in notes). Pedro also asked for a bug fix: the right panel's header and hairlines must line up with the reader bar (A−/A+, RV1909).

Nothing animates under `prefers-reduced-motion`.

## 2026-10-20 · «Objeciones» is its own page in the sidebar (Pedro chose)
**Chosen:** option 1 of https://claude.ai/artifact/Ba4iz7c78D8wXr4p8MEr3Y. Under each tradition, «Objeciones» opens its own page, an index of the objections to that tradition's position («Objeciones a la posición católica»); it takes the normal selected state and stays marked as ancestor while an objection page (04) is open. In the tradition page (03) the «Objeciones» section shows a few objections plus «Ver las N objeciones», like «Ver los N pasajes». The page has the tradition switcher (Pedro, 2026-10-20), keeping the reader on the objections page of the tradition picked. Replaces in part 2026-10-14 ("no separate objections page"); the breadcrumb's «Objeciones» now leads to this page.

Research (`docs/research/sidebar-sections.md`, 12 sites): 7 make a group its own overview page (Docusaurus, Next.js, Stripe, GitBook, Vercel, Mintlify, Sefaria); no site mixes page and section links at one level and highlights the sections. Making «Objeciones» a page puts it on the same footing as «Pasajes».
